# Cars changelog

## cars: measure lot, add TurningRadius and PlayMode bay test
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/{TurningRadius,CarHandlingConfig}.cs, Assets/_Project/Tests/EditMode/Cars/TurningRadiusTests.cs, Assets/_Project/Tests/PlayMode/{UncannyValet.Tests.PlayMode.asmdef,BayEntryDriver.cs,BayEntryTests.cs} (+ .meta)
Why: docs/redesign/car_handling_spec.md section 8 step 1. Measurements (Unity units): drive aisle = clear gap between the bay rows = 4.6 (LotConfig 4.6; LotGeometry puts the top row bottom at y -1.9 and the bottom row top at y -6.5, so the gap equals the config); bays 2.6 x 4.4, pitch 3.2. Car: CarDefinition 1 x 2; the BoxCollider2D is 1 x 1 scaled by the car transform = 1.0 x 2.0; the visible sprite is scaled so its length is 2.0 and its width is 1.25 * 2 / (2.0 to 2.125) = about 1.18 to 1.25 (regular and EV sized the same way). The collider matches the length and is about 0.1 per side narrower than the sprite: not a clear mismatch, so no collider change. The spec's 1.8 x 4 car does not exist; the real car is 1 x 2 (queue slots assume 2.2 x 4.5). EV bay id = 7 (confirmed with IsChargingSpot(7)). R_min = 4.6 / 2 + 1 = 3.3; MaxTurnRate = 4 / 3.3 = 1.212 rad/s (69.5 deg/s). Pull-out rule A >= R_min + w/2 + 0.3 needs 4.1 (collider width 1.0) or 4.225 (sprite 1.25) and the aisle is 4.6: PASS, no aisle change. Bay slack (2.6 - w) / 2 = 0.8 per side (collider) or 0.7 (sprite). CarHandlingConfig holds TurnFullSpeed, MeasuredAisleWidth, radius margin and pull-out clearance with derived RMin and MaxTurnRate (not wired to any car). PlayMode: new assembly UncannyValet.Tests.PlayMode with the section 3 bay-entry test driven through BayEntryDriver (SetInput only). With today's CarDriver the test fails, so the 7 bay cases are [Ignore]d ("enabled in step 2"): at 2 u/s the car turns about 100 deg/s (radius about 1.2, tighter than R_min 3.3), ends about 1.6 units left of the bay centre with its body about 0.9 outside the bay edge, and for bays 3 and 4 also touches the neighbouring parked car (heading error up to 7 deg). One PlayMode measurement test runs and passes.
Tests: EditMode 863/863 passed (10 new TurningRadius and config tests, baseline 853). PlayMode batch (Unity.exe -batchmode -nographics -projectPath . -runTests -testPlatform PlayMode -testResults playmode.xml -logFile playmode.log) exit 0: 1 passed, 7 ignored.

## cars: heavy cars, letter labels, timer fill overlay
Date: 2026-11-06
Files: Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/{CarDefinition,CarDriver,CarFactory,CarLabel,CarLabelMath}.cs, new Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/{CarSorting,CarTimerFill,CarTimerFillMath}.cs (+ .meta), Assets/_Project/Data/Cars/Sedan.asset, Assets/_Project/Tests/EditMode/Cars/{CarLabelMathTests,CarTimerFillMathTests}.cs (+ .meta)
Why: design.md section 10 pressure rules. (1) Heavy cars: CarDefinition gains Mass (20), ParkedLinearDrag and ParkedAngularDrag (8). The body stays Dynamic so spot/curb triggers are untouched (switching body type was rejected as riskier). Mass is set on the Rigidbody2D and damping is applied only while no driver is seated; seated damping is 0 so handling is unchanged. CarDriver sets velocity directly from CarPhysicsMath, so driving does not depend on mass and no force scaling was needed. (2) CarLabel is now a component subscribed to CarLetterAssigned (OnEnable/OnDisable), filtered by CarId, blank until assigned. (3) CarTimerFill listens to TicketTimerChanged/Expired/Stopped for its car and eases a bottom-up overlay (shared cached 1x1 sprite, child scaled in car-local space, park dark, pickup red) using CarTimerFillMath; sorting is body 10, fill 11, label 12 (CarSorting).
Tests: 8 new EditMode tests (CarTimerFillMath x6, CarLabelMath.LetterText x2); Unity not run, compiled by inspection.

## cars: drive-in/drive-out cars and wipe-away mud
Date: 2026-11-06
Files: Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/{CarFactory,CarDefinition,CarInteraction,CarMudMath,CarMudOverlay}.cs, new Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/{CarAutoDrive,CarAutoDriveMath,CarAutoDriveStyle}.cs (+ .meta), Assets/_Project/Tests/EditMode/Cars/{CarLooksMathTests,CarAutoDriveMathTests}.cs (+ .meta)
Why: design.md sections 8-10 (arrival, departure, dirty look). SpawnArriving starts the car EntryDistance behind the stop point and CarAutoDrive eases it in (sqrt braking profile, kinematic MovePosition in FixedUpdate); DriveAwayAndDespawn accelerates it out along its heading and despawns it, and is safe when repeated or for a missing id. While auto-driving, the collider, CarDriver and CarInteraction are disabled (no shoving, no trigger events, no Enter prompt); on arrival the body returns to Dynamic. Tunables are in CarDefinition.AutoDrive. Mud is dark brown (0.17,0.09,0.04,0.9) at constant opacity; washing shrinks the overlay's local X from the left edge (right edge fixed), a left-to-right wipe distinct from the bottom-up timer fill, with no allocation and no renderer.material. CarMudMath.Alpha is replaced by Width/CenterX.
Tests: 7 new or changed EditMode tests (CarAutoDriveMath x5, mud wipe x2); full EditMode run 287/287 passed.

## cars: drift and steering models, Drift input, handling tuning
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/{DriftModel,DriftSettings,SteeringModel,DriveInput,IDriveModifier,DriveModifierStack,CarHandlingLive,CarHandlingTuningLink}.cs (new), Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/{CarDriver,CarFactory,CarHandlingConfig,CarDefinition,CarPhysicsMath}.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Core/Contracts/{IInputSource,IDrivable,TuningCatalog,HandlingRows}.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Core/Events/TuningKey.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Valet/{KeyboardInputSource,SeatController}.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Bootstrap/{GatedInputSource,TuningSeeder,GameBootstrap,HandlingAisleCheck}.cs, Assets/_Project/Editor/{HandlingAssetSetup,BootstrapSceneBuilder}.cs, Assets/_Project/Data/Cars/{CarHandlingConfig,Sedan}.asset, Assets/_Project/Scenes/Bootstrap.unity, tests (Cars/{DriftModelTests,SteeringModelTests,DriveModifierStackTests,CarHandlingTuningTests,CarPhysicsMathTests}, Integration/{HandlingAisleCheckTests,GatedInputSourceTests,FakeCarFactory}, PlayMode/{BayEntryTests,BayEntryDriver})
Why: car_handling_spec.md rollout step 2. CarDriver now runs input modifiers, DriftModel (velocity lags the facing; speed-dependent grip; Shift handbrake) then throttle/brake and SteeringModel (turn rate = MaxTurnRate * steer * clamp01(vF / TurnFullSpeed), so the radius is R_min 3.3 below 4 u/s). IInputSource gains Drift (Left Shift, gamepad right shoulder; one read in KeyboardInputSource, shared with the later dash) and IDrivable gains SetDrift, fed by SeatController. CarHandlingConfig holds every section 2 value; CarHandlingLive is the shared runtime copy that CarHandlingTuningLink updates from the new F2 group "Car handling" (8 advanced rows). The aisle width is copied from LotConfig by HandlingAssetSetup in the scene build and HandlingAisleCheck logs an error at start if they differ. CarDefinition.Grip and TurnRate and the two CarPhysicsMath functions they fed are removed. Deviation from the spec text: "speed removed this step" is the speed magnitude the sideways damping costs (hypot), not the sideways speed itself; the literal version added ForwardGrip * v * turn rate of forward speed in every corner (about +4 u/s per second at 6 u/s full lock), so ForwardGrip 1 now keeps the speed exactly and 0 scrubs it. Slip angle after 2 s of full lock without throttle: about 12 deg at 6 u/s, about 43 deg with Shift. BayEntryTests: removed the Ignore, and the teardown now destroys the spawned cars (they leaked into the next case and were hit by the approaching car).
Tests: EditMode 895/895 passed (new: DriftModel 19, SteeringModel 7, DriveModifierStack 5, CarHandlingTuning 5, HandlingAisleCheck 2; 6 old physics tests removed); PlayMode 8/8 passed (7 bay entries and the geometry check).

## cars: bouncy car-on-car collisions with a bump sound
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/{CarBounceRule,CarBumpLimiter,CarCollisionResponse,CarHandlingKit}.cs (new), Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/{CarDriver,CarFactory,CarHandlingConfig}.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Core/Events/CarEvents.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Audio/{SfxId,SfxAlerts,SfxRecipeBook,SfxEventRouter}.cs, Assets/_Project/Data/Cars/CarHandlingConfig.asset, Assets/_Project/Scenes/Bootstrap.unity, tests (EditMode Cars/CarBounceRuleTests, Audio/CarBumpSfxTests, PlayMode/CarBounceTests)
Why: the human asked for bouncing collisions between cars. An explicit rule, not a PhysicsMaterial2D, because Box2D restitution would also bounce cars off walls and cannot treat braked parked cars as heavy. CarCollisionResponse (one per car) runs in OnCollisionEnter2D and replaces the car's own speed along the contact normal with a 1D restitution result computed from both cars' pre-impact velocities (CarDriver.PreSolveVelocity, the velocity set before the physics step), so mass differences (EV 1.5x later) work. A car with nobody in it counts ParkedMassScale (8) times heavier: the driven car rebounds, the parked one only shuffles and settles under its drag. Rule for who takes part: both cars must have an enabled CarDriver and a Dynamic body. CarAutoDrive and trips switch the body to Kinematic, the collider off and the driver off, so arriving, departing and queued cars neither trigger nor suffer the response and are not jittered; walls, the valet and pedestrians are unchanged. CarsCollided(WorldX, WorldY, Strength01) is a new additive Core event published by the lower-id car of the pair, once per pair per 0.25 s (CarBumpLimiter), only above 0.5 u/s approach speed; strength is approach speed 0.5..6 mapped to 0..1. Audio: SfxId.CarBump, a 0.25 s falling sine thud plus a muffled knock (SfxAlerts.CarBump), SfxEventRouter plays it with its own 0.1 s SfxRateLimiter. Values (CarHandlingConfig): Bounciness 0.4, MinImpactSpeed 0.5, FullImpactSpeed 6, ParkedMassScale 8, BumpPairInterval 0.25. A driven car hitting a parked one at 3 u/s rebounds about 0.7 u/s and shoves it about 0.06 units.
Tests: EditMode 910/910 passed (new: CarBounceRule and limiter 12, bump sfx 3); PlayMode 11/11 passed (3 new: rebound plus event plus settle, gentle touch ignored, non-live car ignored). Not verified by hand in play: the sound level and the feel of the rebound.

## cars: skid marks and tyre audio
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/SlipReading.cs (new), Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/Fx/{SkidConfig,SkidSettings,SkidState,SkidRule,SkidEmitter,DecalLedger,DecalPool,MarkSegment,TyreTrail,TyreMarkMath,SlipMeter}.cs (new), Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/{CarDriver,CarFactory,CarHandlingConfig,CarHandlingLive,DriftModel}.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Core/Contracts/{ISlipSource,HandlingRows}.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Core/Events/{CarEvents,TuningKey}.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Audio/{SfxTyres,SquealLoop,SquealMix,TyreAudio}.cs (new), Assets/_Project/Scripts/Audio/{AudioConfig,AudioDirector,SfxEventRouter,SfxId,SfxRecipeBook}.cs, Assets/_Project/Data/Cars/CarHandlingConfig.asset, tests (EditMode Cars/{SkidRuleTests,DecalLedgerTests,TyreMarkTests,CarHandlingTuningTests}, Audio/TyreSoundTests; PlayMode/SkidMarkTests)
Why: car_handling_spec.md section 6 and lead notes 7 and 12 (rollout step 3). CarDriver exposes a read-only SlipReading (vF, vL, throttle, Shift) per FixedUpdate, taken before that step's damping; SkidRule (pure, one per car) turns it into sliding (|vL| > 1.2, or Shift above 2 u/s; ends below 75% of the threshold), intensity from DriftModel's slip angle (40 deg = full) and mark opacity (30% to 80%), and a hard-brake start (throttle against travel above 4 u/s). SkidEmitter is a thin shell on every car, active only with a seated driver and an enabled CarDriver (trip and auto-driven cars never emit): it drops a stroke behind each rear tyre every 1.0 unit of tyre travel, lying from the tyre's previous to its current position (the true travel direction), into DecalPool (cap 40, fade 6 s, oldest recycled, per-instance colour with a tint parameter for blood, shared 1x1 sprite, sorting order -3: above asphalt -25/-24 and route and curb arrows -5/-4, below traffic 1, pedestrians 4 and cars 9+). Events (additive, Core): CarSlideStarted, CarSlideEnded, CarHardBraked. The squeal reads slip strength through the new Core contract ISlipSource (IsSliding, Intensity01), polled by Audio each frame: no event per frame and no allocation; CarFactory registers its SlipMeter in Services at Start (Bootstrap is not edited). Audio: SfxId.TyreChirp (0.15 s rising high chirp, rate limited 0.25 s) on CarSlideStarted, SfxId.TyreScreech (0.45 s, rate limited 0.6 s) on CarHardBraked, and a 0.4 s seamless squeal loop (whole cycles per tone, circular high-passed hiss) on a dedicated AudioSource driven by TyreAudio and the pure SquealMix (volume 0.25..0.8 and pitch 0.9..1.5 with intensity, 0.03 s attack, 0.15 s fade-out, fades while paused). F2 keys under Car handling (advanced): slide threshold, Shift skid speed, mark darkness, mark fade time; the other skid numbers (SkidConfig, serialized inside CarHandlingConfig so no new asset) and the decal cap are fixed. Mark spacing is 1.0 unit and stroke width 0.07 (one art pixel) because a 40-decal cap with short strokes would recycle in under a second.
Tests: EditMode 948/948 passed (31 new: SkidRule 11, decals and tyre trail 10, slip meter 1, tuning 1, audio 8); PlayMode 14/14 passed (3 new: driven slide leaves marks and reports the slide, slide ends when the driver gets out, driverless car never emits). Not verified by hand: mark visibility at zoom 0.78, sound character, loop seam in WebGL.

## cars: sharper turning, live drive scales, softer tyre and bump sounds
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Cars/{CarHandlingConfig,CarHandlingLive,CarDriver,TurningRadius}.cs, Cars/DriveSettings.cs (new), Core/Contracts/{HandlingRows,TuningCatalog}.cs, Core/Events/TuningKey.cs, Bootstrap/{HandlingAisleCheck,TuningSeeder,GameBootstrap}.cs, Audio/{SoftenFilter,TyreSoundTuning}.cs (new), Audio/{SfxTyres,SquealLoop,SfxAlerts,SfxBank,SfxRecipeBook,AudioConfig,AudioDirector,TyreAudio}.cs, Data/Cars/CarHandlingConfig.asset, tests (Cars/{TurningRadiusTests,CarHandlingTuningTests}, Audio/TyreSoundTests)
Why: play-test notes. (1) The tightest turn radius is a direct key ('Tightest turn radius', default 2.2, was derived 3.3 from the aisle); MaxTurnRate = TurnFullSpeed / radius = 1.82 rad/s (104 deg/s); above 4 u/s the radius still grows as speed / MaxTurnRate (8 u/s gives 4.4). MeasuredAisleWidth and HandlingAisleCheck stay as warnings (width mismatch, or radius above aisle/2 + margin via TurningRadius.CanEnterBay). Bay-entry PlayMode tests still pass. (2) Driving values are live multipliers (0.5..2, default 1) on each CarDefinition value, applied in CarHandlingLive.DriveFor: multipliers keep per-car differences and the later EV overrides intact. Keys: top speed, acceleration, braking, coasting slow-down (0.25..3), reverse speed (capped so reverse never exceeds the top speed). Seeded through CarHandlingLive.Seed (already called by TuningSeeder). (3) Sounds softened: tyre chirp 1900-2700 Hz sine+triangle with a hiss, 90 ms, to 600-850 Hz pure sine, 60 ms, 10 ms attack, no hiss; screech 1250-900 Hz saw with hiss, 300 ms, to 480-360 Hz triangle with 35% saw, filtered 1200-700 Hz, 30 ms attack, 250 ms, no hiss; squeal loop 1000/1502/2005 Hz plus strong hiss to 500/752 Hz, the 1000 Hz partial and a little grit scaled by sharpness; every tyre sound gets a one-pole low-pass whose cutoff runs 500 Hz (sharpness 0) to 4000 Hz (1), default 0.35 = about 1.7 kHz. Squeal volume 0.25..0.8 to 0.12..0.4. Car bump: thud attack 2 to 12 ms, knock square to triangle with a 400 Hz filter (was 700), whole sound low-passed at 1100 Hz. Pedestrian boing: 170-340 Hz to 130-230 Hz, attack 3 to 12 ms, low-passed at 1100 Hz. New F2 keys 'Tyre sound volume' (default 1, also scales chirp, squeal and screech; the one-shots sit at 0.6 of the effect volume) and 'Tyre sound sharpness' (default 0.35); the panel re-renders the tyre clips on change. They are seeded by TuningSeeder from AudioConfig (new optional parameter).
Tests: EditMode 958/958 passed (10 new: radius, drive scales, dullness by zero-crossing rate and brightness ratio, sharpness ordering, seam at 3 sharpness values, tuning link); PlayMode 14/14 passed (bay entries unchanged). Not verified by ear.
## cars: make road trip cars solid and let them yield
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/{CarAutoDrive,CarAutoDriveMath,CarAutoDriveStyle,CarBounceRule,CarCollisionResponse,CarFactory,CarTrip}.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Cars/TripProbe.cs (new), Assets/_Project/Scripts/Core/Contracts/ITripObstacles.cs (new, additive), Assets/_Project/Scripts/Traffic/{TripObstacle (new),ITrip,EntryTrip,ExitTrip,TripRouter,TripTuning,TrafficConfig,TrafficModule}.cs, tests (EditMode Traffic/TripObstacleTests, Cars/CarBounceRuleTests; PlayMode/TripCarSolidTests)
Why: play-test note "no collision when a car is incoming into the queue". One-off cross-module edit (Cars + Traffic) approved by the lead. A road trip car (entry or exit) now keeps its collider on while the body stays Kinematic and pose driven, so the player's car, the valet and other cars cannot pass through it and it is never shoved. Straight arrive/depart (no road) still run with the collider off. CarCollisionResponse treats a kinematic trip car as an immovable car (CarBounceRule.ImmovableMass): the live car reflects off it (restitution), the live car publishes CarsCollided and the bump sound. Yielding: CarAutoDrive casts its own collider along its heading every FixedUpdate (TripProbe, Rigidbody2D.Cast into a reused buffer; distance 3 + 0.9 s * speed, CarAutoDriveStyle LookAheadBase/LookAheadSeconds) and reports the bumper gap to the nearest non-trigger body that has a rigidbody (cars, valet; pedestrians are masked out, static scenery ignored, only surfaces facing the heading count) through a new additive Core contract ITripObstacles (implemented by TripRouter beside ITripDriver). The trips cap their speed with TripObstacle: CarFollowing.TargetSpeed against a stationary leader, so the car brakes to MinGap behind it at the follow Brake rate and the cap lifts as soon as the way is clear. Timeout rule: once held stopped for TripTuning.BlockedTimeout (6 s, TrafficConfig range 1..30) the cap becomes CreepSpeed (0.8 u/s, range 0.1..3) until the way is clear, so it never waits forever (the creep nudges a dynamic obstacle on). Entry lane and exit lane cars use the same cap and lane occupancy is updated with their real speed (LaneMotion), so ambient traffic behind a stopped patron car follows it normally. Queue slot re-targeting, the single CarArrived at the route end and the exit merge yield are unchanged. Factory order fix: CarAutoDrive is added before CarCollisionResponse (which looks it up).
Tests: EditMode 959/959 passed (11 new: obstacle cap, timeout creep, reset, blocked entry holds and resumes, permanently blocked entry still arrives, blocked exit stops, immovable bounce, look-ahead); PlayMode 16/16 passed (2 new: driven car pushed into a trip car rebounds and the trip car's position is unchanged; trip car stops behind a parked car, resumes when it is removed and arrives exactly once). Not verified by hand in play: feel of the stop distances, creep after timeout in the real lot, interaction with the real slot scan.

## cars: hard impacts damage cars and lower the tip [Claude]
Date: 2026-11-12
Files: Cars/{DamageRule,CarDamage}.cs (new), Cars/{CarFactory,CarHandlingConfig,CarHandlingLive,CarHandlingTuningLink}.cs, Core/Events/{DamageEvents (new),TuningKey}.cs, Core/Contracts/{DamageRows (new),TuningCatalog}.cs, Patrons/{DamageLedger (new),TicketSettler,TicketRegistry}.cs, UI/{TicketRow,TicketNeedsTracker}.cs, UI/Hud/{DamageTag (new),KeyBoardPainter}.cs, tests (EditMode Cars/DamageRuleTests, Patrons/DamageTipTests, UI/DamageTagTests; PlayMode/CarDamageTests)
Why: design.md A8 tip section and the chaos redesign damage meter (issue 19). Pure DamageRule: impact force (contact impulse over the car's mass, a speed change in units/s) gives damage 0 up to the threshold, then percent per unit, capped at 1. CarDamage (added by CarFactory) reads OnCollisionEnter2D normalImpulse without allocating: walls and station edges and other cars count (both cars of a crash, parked and queued cars too); the pedestrian layer and any other body never do. It publishes CarImpact(car, force, isPlayerCar) for each counted hit (for screen shake) and CarDamaged(car, total01, delta01), and resets on ShiftStarted. Patrons' DamageLedger keeps damage per car, feeds TipInput.Damage01 at payout (25% off per 25% damage, $0 Wrecked at 100%) and clears on pay, void and shift start. The key board shows "DMG nn%" in its own column only when a listed car has damage (panel 54 px wider only then). New appended F2 Advanced keys DamageMinForce (1.5) and DamagePercentPerForce (8) live in CarHandlingConfig and seed through CarHandlingLive.Seed (no seeder or scene change).
Tests: EditMode 1314/1314 passed; PlayMode 29/29 passed (3 new: two cars at speed damage both, a wall damages, a tap and the pedestrian layer do not; BootstrapSceneSmokeTests passes).

## director-2: archetype picker, dilemma rules and gate, soak test
Date: 2026-11-09
Files:
- Core (additive): DilemmaKind (enum); events DilemmaCreated, DilemmaResolved, ReturnForwardWanted (DirectorEvents); DecisionKind.DilemmaResolved; DirectorDecision (optional HasRule, Rule, Boost, ResolveSeconds); IDirectorView (HasOpenDilemma, OpenDilemma, DilemmaCooldownLeft, DilemmasMade, DilemmasResolved, AverageResolveSeconds, ReturnForwardWanted); 19 TuningKey entries (DirDilemma*, DirLotTightSpots, DirSolvablePickupSeconds, DirHeadroomGap, DirLoad*, DirStreakLimit, DirMixTolerance, DirAssist, DirAssistDilemmaScale) and their TuningCatalog rows under "Director".
- Director (new): IDilemmaRule, DilemmaBoost, WashBlockedRule, WashBusyRule, BaysBlockedRule, BaySquattedRule, LastBayRule, LotTightRule, ChainRule, DilemmaRules (rule list), DilemmaSolvable; DilemmaGate, DilemmaDirector, DilemmaContext; ArchetypePicker, PickRequest, PickResult, ArchetypeWeights, ArchetypeKinds, ArchetypeHistory, MixLimit; DilemmaRuleSettings, GateSettings, PickerSettings; IIntensityProfile.
- Director (changed): DirectorBrain (picks the archetype through DilemmaDirector, takes IIntensityProfile and a DilemmaDirector, assist clamps form F at 0), PatronDirector (builds the picker, gate and rules), DirectorState (dilemma counters), DirectorSettings and DirectorConfig (three new sections), DirectorProfile (Weights, IsRising, rising lookahead field; implements IIntensityProfile), PacerInput (Gap), ArrivalPacer (uses Gap).
- UI: DirectorOverlay and DirectorOverlayText (open dilemma and rule, cooldown, counters, decision lines with the boost reason, resolved entries).
- Patrons: no change needed. PatronSpawner already honours PatronScheduled.ArchetypeChosen through PatronRequestRoller.Roll(progress, archetype) and ArchetypeTraits; the old plan chances run only when the director is off.
- Tests (EditMode): new DilemmaRuleTests, DilemmaGateTests, DilemmaDirectorTests, ArchetypePickerTests, ArchetypeBoostTests, DirectorSoakTests with helpers PickerTestKit, SoakProfile, SoakCar, SoakSim, SoakSimPlacement, SoakLotView, SoakShift; updated DirectorBrainTests (chosen archetype, boosted dilemma), DirectorFakes (TestRandom, NextPickup), DirectorOverlayTextTests, DirectorProfileTests, Integration/TuningSeederTests.
Why: patron_director_spec.md sections 4 and 5 and rollout step 3: weighted archetype draw over the unlocked stations, load scaling by the pacing band, seven dilemma rules behind IDilemmaRule, the dilemma budget (one open, 25 s cooldown, headroom, never in relief, solvable, 20 s blackout after an angry patron), streak limit, bounded mix, assist hook, one seeded RNG, and the 200-shift soak test.
Tests: compile-checked outside Unity (all of Scripts and Tests compiled together against the Unity 6000.2.5f1 reference assemblies, no errors). The new pure fixtures (DilemmaRuleTests, DilemmaGateTests, DilemmaDirectorTests, ArchetypePickerTests, ArchetypeBoostTests, DirectorSoakTests, DirectorOverlayTextTests) were also run with a scratch NUnit-style runner: 50 passed, 0 failed, the 200-shift soak in about 0.7 s. Not run: fixtures that need the native Unity engine (DirectorBrainTests, DirectorProfileTests, TuningSeederTests, which create ScriptableObjects). New .meta files are not committed; Unity creates them on first import.

=== docs/changelogs/director.md ===
# Changelog: director

## director: always a car in the arrival queue (idle guard) [Claude]
Date: 2026-11-11
Files: Scripts/Director/{IdleGuard,ArrivalPacer,PacerInput,PacingSettings,DirectorBrain}.cs, Scripts/Core/Contracts/{ArrivalRows,TuningCatalog}.cs, Scripts/Core/Events/TuningKey.cs, Data/Director/DirectorConfig.asset, Tests/EditMode/Director/{IdleGuardTests,ArrivalPacerTests,ArrivalPacerStuckTests,DirectorBrainTests,DirectorSoakTests,SoakShift}.cs, Tests/EditMode/Integration/DifficultyDialsTests.cs
Why: Issue #70, no more than about 2 s with an empty arrival queue. New pure IdleGuard counts seconds with no car awaiting park; at the limit the pacer sends one regardless of its countdown or relief hold (a relief send is plain). A queued car resets it, so normal pacing, mercy and the queue cap are untouched. It stands down when the arrival budget is spent or fewer than ArrivalIdleCutoff (20 s) remain. The first car of a shift is due after one limit. A lost send is retried within the limit while the queue is empty. Keys ArrivalIdleLimit (2 s, visible) and ArrivalIdleCutoff (advanced) are seeded by PacingSettings, not by a dial. F3 "next in" shows the guard countdown.
Tests: 11 new in IdleGuardTests, 1 in DirectorBrainTests; soak asserts the empty-queue gap and first car within limit + 1 s over 200 shifts (longest observed 1.5 s). EditMode 1111/1111, PlayMode 16/16 passed. RunBatch run; Bootstrap.unity churn reverted.

## director: return scheduler, late-shift pickups, arrival cutoff [Claude]
Date: 2026-11-11
Files: Scripts/Director/{ReturnScheduler,LateReturnPlanner,ReturnCandidates,ReturnCandidate,ReturnDirector,ReturnInput,ReturnShift,ReturnSettings,DirectorBrain,DirectorConfig,DirectorSettings,PatronDirector,ArrivalPacer,HoldReason,PacingSettings,CarBook,CarRecord,SnapshotBuilder,LotStateTracker}.cs, Scripts/Patrons/{ReturnShifter,Ticket,TicketRegistry}.cs, Scripts/Core/{Events/DirectorEvents,Events/TuningKey,Contracts/ReturnRows,Contracts/TuningCatalog,Contracts/ArrivalRows,Contracts/CarSnapshot}.cs, Data/Director/DirectorConfig.asset, Tests/EditMode/{Director,Patrons}/*
Why: Issues #12, #13, #73, #15; spec section 5. Pure ReturnScheduler keeps returns in the +/-25% window, spaces pickups 4 s, nudges one return forward or back per cooldown, and from 60 s before the end pulls every return forward outside the window. Director asks via ReturnShiftRequested, Patrons executes (ReturnShifter, Ticket.ShiftStay) and answers PatronReturnShifted. ArrivalIdleCutoff (now 45 s) also holds paced arrivals. New keys ReturnWindowPercent, PickupSpacingSeconds, LatePickupStart, LastPickupLead.
Tests: EditMode 1214/1214, PlayMode 16/16 after merging pivot/chaos (#77 stay clock from drop-off: the scheduler counts every open ticket wherever its car is). RunBatch run; Bootstrap.unity churn reverted. Soak over 200 shifts: 2325 arrivals, 2314 pickups requested, 0 moves outside the window.

## lot: stay starts when a car first rests clear of the queue [Claude]
Date: 2026-11-11
Files: Core: Events/{CarEvents,TicketEvents,TuningKey}.cs, Contracts/{QueueRows,TuningCatalog}.cs. Lot: QueueClearRule, QueueClearRunner, QueueClearSettings, LotConfig, LotLayoutBuilder. Patrons: Ticket, StayStarter (new), TicketRegistry, ServiceTuning, PatronConfig, PatronTuning, LiveTicketTuning. Director: CarRecord, CarBook, LotStateTracker, SnapshotBuilder, ReturnCandidates, LateReturnPlanner (docs). Bootstrap: TuningSeeder, GameBootstrap (seed the new distance). Tests: StayStartTests, QueueClearRuleTests, TicketReturnTests, TicketRegistry*Tests, LotStateTrackerTests, ShiftHarness, Soak* (fake lot).
Why: Issue #78 (Refs #78), closes the loophole left by #77 where a car left in the queue could be handed back fast. The patron stay now starts the first time the car rests (no driver, slow, settle time) at least QueueClearDistance (default 6 units, from the nearest queue slot centre) from the queue, or when it is parked in a bay, or after QueueMaxWaitMinutes (default 3 game minutes) in the queue. New Core events CarClearedQueue (Lot) and StayStarted (Patrons, for the Director); TuningKey QueueClearDistance and QueueMaxWaitMinutes appended at the end; F2 rows in QueueRows (Advanced). A ticket whose stay has not started has no ReturnMinute (infinity), is not summoned and cannot be shifted; the Director sees infinite SecondsToPickup, so it is neither a return candidate nor in the late phase until its stay starts (the wait limit bounds that).
Tests: new StayStartTests (6) and QueueClearRuleTests (5); returns and registry tests now start the stay with CarClearedQueue; soak fake lot starts the stay at placement or the queue limit, all 200 seeds pass. EditMode 1226/1226, PlayMode 17/17 (incl. BootstrapSceneSmokeTests); RunBatch run, no scene change needed (QueueClearRunner is added at runtime by LotLayoutBuilder).

Debt left = max(0, debt at NightStarted - tips), independent of DebtLedger order; tickets closing after NightEnded are ignored so nothing double counts.

RunLostCard shows on the final strike and freezes the game with Time.timeScale 0; its New run button calls a handler set via Configure, which is NOT wired yet: NextNightRequested cannot be reused because NightFlow ignores it while a night runs and nothing ends the night on the final strike (lead decision). Scene must be rebuilt (Build Everything) to add the two new children; until then GameBootstrap logs an error and skips them. Orders is not wired.
Tests: added MessageFeedTests (issue, flash, each outcome, cap, cancel, reset on night 1, strikes); not run, the sandbox blocked launching Unity. TuningSeeder tests may fail on the four unseeded TuningKey values (expected).

## ui: JetBrains Mono UI font, title is Valet to Valhalla, Refs #168 #50 [Claude]
Date: 2026-11-17
Files: Assets/_Project/Scripts/UI/Kit/ (new: UiFontConfig, UiFontSizing, PixelFontRenderer; PixelCanvas, PixelKit, PixelGlyphs edited: bitmap atlas and glyph rows removed); UI/Screens/TitlePainter.cs; Assets/_Project/Art/Fonts/ (JetBrainsMono-Bold.ttf, OFL.txt); Assets/_Project/Resources/UiFontConfig.asset; Tests/EditMode/UI/UiFontTests.cs (new), PixelKitTests.cs; ProjectSettings/ProjectSettings.asset (productName); README.md; tools/write-build-info.sh (+ .meta files)
Why: #168 all UI text now draws with a real TrueType font instead of the 5x7 bitmap. Font: JetBrainsMono-Bold.ttf from https://github.com/JetBrains/JetBrainsMono (fonts/ttf), licence SIL Open Font License 1.1 (OFL.txt kept beside it), 274 KB, embedded in the build. It is monospaced (advance 0.6 em, capitals 0.73 em), so each letter is centred in the same 6x7 virtual-pixel cell as before: every layout, box and wrap width is unchanged and no label can overflow where it fitted before. Font size is asked in screen pixels (capitals one row tall) per grid scale and text scale, so letters are crisp at any window size; sizes snap to steps while pops zoom, and cap at 96 px. Letters are cached one-char strings drawn through a reused GUIStyle/GUIContent: no per-frame allocation. Size and nudge tunables are in Resources/UiFontConfig (_emPerRow, _maxFontSize, _zoomSizeStep, _verticalNudge). Still upper case only; colours untouched. #50 the title screen reads VALET TO / VALHALLA (two lines, each fits at scale 7); productName, README title and the build-info text renamed. site/ not rebuilt.
Tests: EditMode 2007 passed (UiFontTests: sizing maths, shipped font monospaced and every glyph within the text cell, title fits the canvas). Not checked visually in a Web build; PlayMode not run.

## lot-v4: traffic defaults from the F2 tuning
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Scripts/Traffic/{TrafficConfig,TrafficModule}.cs, Scripts/Bootstrap/TuningSeeder.cs, Data/Traffic/TrafficConfig.asset, Tests/EditMode/Integration/TuningSeederTests.cs
Why: human tuned traffic in F2 (design.md A5): ambient 13 cars per minute, ambient speed scale 1.25, entry car speed 7.3, exit car speed 5 (units per second, as the panel shows). New TrafficConfig fields EntrySpeed and ExitSpeed hold the start values; TuningSeeder seeds the keys from them and TrafficModule applies them at build. CruiseSpeed and RouteSpeed stay the scale-1 references (and still drive ambient cars), so the panel value is the real speed in units per second.
Tests: TuningSeederTests asserts the four defaults.

## lot-v4: waiting patrons always face the pickup zone
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Scripts/Pedestrians/{WalkAnimMath,WalkerRouter,PatronWalkers}.cs, Tests/EditMode/Pedestrians/WalkAnimMathTests.cs
Why: human play test (design.md A5, image 6): some patrons at the curb faced down. Root cause: they turned toward the zone centre point and FacingToward lets the dominant axis win, so a patron standing well above or below the (tall) zone centre faced up or down along the curb instead of across to it. Now the look point is the centre x at the patron own height (WalkAnimMath.CurbLookPoint), so they face straight across, and the facing is set in WalkerRouter.Apply for the Waiting phase, so any re-apply of that phase restores it, not only the arrival moment.
Tests: WalkAnimMathTests.CurbLookPoint_FacesAcrossToTheZoneHoweverFarUpOrDownItsCentreIs (regression; shows the old rule gave Up).

## lot-v4: key board lists a car only once it is parked
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Scripts/UI/{TicketRow,TicketBoard,GameUi}.cs, Scripts/UI/Hud/{KeyBoardRows,KeyBoardPainter}.cs, Tests/EditMode/UI/TicketBoardTests.cs
Why: human request (design.md A5 key board): a car in the arrival queue or being driven no longer shows on the key board; it appears when CarParked (spot) or CarEnteredStation (wash or charger) fires and stays until the ticket closes (delivered). TicketBoard keeps a Placed flag per row (also for a car parked before its ticket opens); the pure KeyBoardRows rule filters the rows for the key board only, other painters still see every ticket.
Tests: TicketBoardTests (not listed on arrival, listed when parked, stays through leaving the spot and the pickup, gone on close, station parking, parked before ticket).

## lot-v4: pickup zone moves right, patrons wait in the gap beside the diner

## lot: stay starts when a car first rests clear of the queue [Claude]

A ticket whose stay has not started has no ReturnMinute (infinity), is not summoned and cannot be shifted; the Director sees infinite SecondsToPickup, so it is neither a return candidate nor in the late phase until its stay starts (the wait limit bounds that).

A new car drives to the end of the queue and a full queue holds the arrival (the arrival gate no longer counts parked-pending tickets, only free queue positions). The patron stays in the car until the valet (on foot or driving) is within the approach range of the car in the pickup spot (PatronStepsOut), then walks to the diner; PatronEnteredDiner at the door starts the stay clock (StayStarter). When the pickup car leaves the spot the car behind drives forward into it (CarRepositioned when it stops), the others follow slot by slot, and the freed tail slot takes the next arrival. A queued car the valet takes anyway lets its patron out at once (no stall). Issue #78's queue-distance rule, the 3 game-minute fallback, CarClearedQueue, ServiceTuning and their F2 rows are deleted; TuningKey QueueClearDistance and QueueMaxWaitMinutes stay as RetiredQueue* (append-only).

The stay and queue-wait tests of #78 were replaced on purpose (StayStartTests, QueueClearRuleTests removed); tests that started the stay at park or CarClearedQueue now publish PatronEnteredDiner.

## lot: perfect drop-off needs the car between the curb arrows [Claude]

## lot: stay starts when a car first rests clear of the queue [Claude]
Date: 2026-11-11
Files: Core: Events/{CarEvents,TicketEvents,TuningKey}.cs, Contracts/{QueueRows,TuningCatalog}.cs. Lot: QueueClearRule, QueueClearRunner, QueueClearSettings, LotConfig, LotLayoutBuilder. Why: Issue #78 (Refs #78), closes the loophole left by #77 where a car left in the queue could be handed back fast. The patron stay now starts the first time the car rests (no driver, slow, settle time) at least QueueClearDistance (default 6 units, from the nearest queue slot centre) from the queue, or when it is parked in a bay, or after QueueMaxWaitMinutes (default 3 game minutes) in the queue. New Core events CarClearedQueue (Lot) and StayStarted (Patrons, for the Director); TuningKey QueueClearDistance and QueueMaxWaitMinutes appended at the end; F2 rows in QueueRows (Advanced). A ticket whose stay has not started has no ReturnMinute (infinity), is not summoned and cannot be shifted; the Director sees infinite SecondsToPickup, so it is neither a return candidate nor in the late phase until its stay starts (the wait limit bounds that).
Tests: new StayStartTests (6) and QueueClearRuleTests (5); returns and registry tests now start the stay with CarClearedQueue; soak fake lot starts the stay at placement or the queue limit, all 200 seeds pass. EditMode 1226/1226, PlayMode 17/17 (incl. BootstrapSceneSmokeTests); RunBatch run, no scene change needed (QueueClearRunner is added at runtime by LotLayoutBuilder).

## patrons: jitter each patron stay so returns spread out [Claude]
Date: 2026-11-16
Files: Patrons/StayJitter.cs (new), PatronSpawner.cs, PatronConfig.cs, PatronTuning.cs; test Patrons/StayJitterTests.cs.
Why: Issue #144 (playtest): return timers ran out one after another and pickup requests came in a clump. Each car stay (rolled 20-45) now moves by a seeded random offset of up to PatronConfig.StayJitterFraction (default 0.3, +/-30%) of itself, never below the late-stay floor. The draw is seeded from the car id, so it needs no extra roll from the shared random and tests stay deterministic. It runs before StayFit.Clamp, so the stay still ends inside the shift; tip maths is untouched (it uses time since the pickup request). No minimum gap added: the stay clock starts per car, so independent offsets already spread returns.
Tests: StayJitterTests (7: varied, bounds, floor, determinism, off at 0, spread, fits shift). EditMode 1894/1894.

## pedestrians: collision, wait-and-resume, rubber bounce and bump sound
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Assets/_Project/Scripts/Pedestrians/{PedestrianWalker,PedestrianCrowd,PedestrianConfig,PathFollower,PedestrianBlockRule,PedestrianBlockState,PedestrianBounceRule,WalkerBody,WalkerSensor}.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Core/PhysicsLayers.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Core/Events/PedestrianEvents.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Patrons/ArrivalSlotScanner.cs, Assets/_Project/Scripts/Audio/{AudioDirector,SfxAlerts,SfxEventRouter,SfxId,SfxRecipeBook,SfxRateLimiter}.cs, ProjectSettings/TagManager.asset, Assets/_Project/Tests/EditMode/Pedestrians/PedestrianBlockRuleTests.cs, Assets/_Project/Tests/EditMode/Audio/SfxTests.cs
Why: design.md Part A (pedestrians). Walkers get a kinematic body and circle collider on layer Pedestrian (8). They look ahead, wait when blocked, then pass through after a timeout so no route freezes. A car or the valet bumping one gives a tiny rubber-ball bounce and a PedestrianBumped event, which plays a short boing. The queue slot scan ignores the Pedestrian layer. Core event and Audio edits were a one-off exception granted by the lead.
Tests: 12 new EditMode tests (block rule, bounce rule, router bump and rate limit); full suite 792 passed, 0 failed.

## pedestrians: patrons ignore their own car (no push, wait or bounce)
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Assets/_Project/Scripts/Pedestrians/{OwnCarRule,WalkerSprites,WalkerBody,WalkerSensor,PedestrianWalker,PatronWalkers}.cs, Assets/_Project/Tests/EditMode/Pedestrians/PedestrianBlockRuleTests.cs
Why: play-test bug: a patron stepping out of the car pushed it sideways. A patron now ignores the colliders of the car tied to their ticket (Physics2D.IgnoreCollision, re-applied when the collider or object is switched back on, released on despawn), the look-ahead skips that car, and a touch from it gives no bounce and no PedestrianBumped. Other cars and the valet behave as before. Sprite layers moved to WalkerSprites to keep PedestrianWalker under 200 lines.

## pedestrians: knocked pedestrians walk on from where they land [Claude]

# pivot-art changelog

## pivot-art: import MetroCity art and add pixel import postprocessor
Date: 2026-11-09
Files: Assets/_Project/Art/ThirdParty/MetroCity/** (29 PNGs + CREDITS.md), Assets/_Project/Editor/Import/{ArtPostprocessor,SlicePlanner,SlicePlan,SpriteSheetBuilder}.cs, docs/changelogs/pivot-art.md
Why: chaos_redesign.md pivot steps 4 and 5. Art extracted from art-src/MetroCity/*.rar (originals untouched; "MetroCity 2.0 4.rar" ignored as a duplicate). Native pixel grid is 32 px, so the project PPU is 32.
Tests: none run (Unity could not be launched). Slice rules are the pure class SlicePlanner; no EditMode test added yet.

### Import settings (all PNGs under Assets/_Project/Art/)
Sprite, PPU 32, Point filter, uncompressed, no mipmaps, clamp, max size 4096 (Building.png is 4096x768).
Re-import an asset to re-apply. The postprocessor lives in the existing UncannyValet.Editor assembly.

### Verified source sizes
Tiles.png 400x288 (irregular atlas, not a clean 32 grid), Road.png 320x320 (10x10 of 32), Tree-Sheet.png 192x64 (4 trees, 48x64 each),
Cars-Sheet.png 1152x512 (9x4 of 128), FireTruck.png 640x160 (4 of 160x160), Character Model.png 768x192 (24x6 of 32),
Hairs.png 768x256 (24x8), Outfit1-6.png 768x32 (24x1), Suit.png 768x128, Suit1.png and Pack20/Hair.png 768x160 (24 columns, 4-5 rows),
Hair1-7.png and Shadow.png 32x32 (single), Environment/Buildings/Building.png 4096x768, Stations/ PNGs single images.

### Sprite names (deterministic)
- Grid sheets: `{file}_r{row}_c{col}`, row 0 = TOP row, col 0 = left. Example `Cars-Sheet_r1_c5`, `Character Model_r0_c3`. Pivot centre.
- Auto-sliced sheets (Tiles.png, Buildings/Building.png): `{file}_{index:00}`, ordered top to bottom (64 px bands) then left to right. Pivot bottom centre.
- Single images (Hair1-7, Shadow, Outfits are grids, Stations/*): sprite name = file name. Stations pivot bottom centre.

### Cars (Art/ThirdParty/MetroCity/Cars)
Cars-Sheet: 9 columns x 4 rows, 128 px cells, each cell is one view of one vehicle (colour variants).
By inspection (a colour tint is baked into the outline of each variant):
- Top-down (tall, use for gameplay): r0 c0,c1,c2(ambulance),c5,c6; r1 c0,c1,c5(green),c6(police); r2 c0,c1,c4,c5,c6(ambulance); r3 c0,c1(police),c4,c5.
- Side/front views (wide, for menus or garage art): all remaining cells; ambulance side is r1 c4 and r3 c8.
Top-down sprites appear to face up (nose toward the top of the cell); the lead should confirm in Unity and set the car rotation offset once.
FireTruck.png: 4 cells of 160: c0 side, c1 top-down, c2 side (mirrored), c3 top-down (alternate colouring).
No damage frames exist; use tint, smoke and shake.

### Characters (Art/ThirdParty/MetroCity/Characters)
All sheets are 32 px cells, 24 columns. Character Model has 6 rows = 6 skin/body variants (row 0 top). Hair, Outfit and Suit sheets use the same column layout so layers can be drawn at the same position:
- cols 0-5: facing down (front), 6 frames
- cols 6-11: side view, first direction
- cols 12-17: facing up (back), 6 frames
- cols 18-23: side view, other direction
Each 6-frame group is a run cycle; frame 0 of a group can serve as the idle pose. Which side group is left or right, and the exact idle frame, were judged from a downscaled preview and must be confirmed in Unity (flip with SpriteRenderer.flipX if both sides look the same).
Hairs.png: 8 hair styles/colours as rows (each row = same 24 columns). Hair is only animated facing forward per the pack notes. Outfits: Outfit1-6, one row each. Suits/Suit.png, Suit1.png and Hair/Pack20/Hair.png are the older 2.0 layers.
Layer by stacking SpriteRenderers (model, outfit, hair) and switching all three to the same r/c frame.

### Environment
- Tiles.png (auto-sliced): grass patches, cobbles, fences, signs, props.
- Road/Road.png (32 px grid): road, crosswalks and kerb pieces; sliced as r{row}_c{col}.
- Trees/Tree-Sheet.png: `Tree-Sheet_r0_c0..c3`, 48x64.
- Buildings/Building.png (auto): 9 buildings with baked shadows. Any may be used for the diner; the tan building with green roof (second row, second from left) is a good fit.
- Stations/: Building.png (176x320 tower house), FireStation.png, PoliceStation.png as single sprites.

## pivot-core: replace nights, debt and boss orders with scored shifts
Date: 2026-11-09
Files:
- Removed: Scripts/Orders (whole module, asmdef, OrdersConfig, StrikeTracker), Tests/EditMode/Orders, Data/Orders; Core IDebtView, NightEvents, NightPlan, NightSummary, OrderEvents, OrderOutcome, OrderId; Economy DebtLedger, NightTally, NightTallyRecorder, RunConfig (+ Data/Economy/RunConfig.asset); Night NightClock, INightEnder, RunLossGuard, NightPlanConfig/Defaults/Entry/Source (+ Data/Night/NightPlanConfig.asset); Bootstrap NightFlow, RunDebt, IRunReset, INightStarter, ClockNightStarter; UI MessagePanel, MessageFeed, MessageFeedSettings, MessageFormatter, MessagePanelStyles, MessageStatus, FeedMessage, NightSummaryCard, SummaryCard*; their EditMode tests.
- Added: Core/Config/ShiftConfig, Core/Contracts/IShiftScoreView, Core/Events/{ShiftEvents,ShiftPlan,ShiftResult}; Night/{ShiftClock,ShiftTimer,ShiftTimerStep,ShiftPlanSource}; Economy/{ShiftScore,ComboTracker,StarRating,DamagePenalty}; Bootstrap/{ShiftFlow,IShiftStarter,ClockShiftStarter}; UI/{ResultsScreen,ResultsFormatter,ResultsContent,ResultsStyles,ScoreLines}.
- Changed: IGameClock (SecondsRemaining, ShiftLengthSeconds, ElapsedMinutes, IsRunning), TipInput/TipBreakdown/TipZeroReason, TipCalculator, TipConfig, EconomyEvents (ScoreChanged, ComplaintFiled replace DebtChanged), TuningKey/TuningCatalog, ClockFormat (m:ss), Patrons (PatronSpawner, PatronPlan, PatronRequestRoller, PatronTuning, PatronConfig arrival 15-25, TicketRegistry, TicketSettler, StationService, event renames), Lot LotExpander/StationSwitch and Audio PendingCarTracker/SfxEventRouter (event renames only), DebugOverlay, GameBootstrap, TuningSeeder, Editor ProjectSetup/BootstrapSceneBuilder, asmdefs (Orders reference dropped), Data/Patrons/PatronConfig.asset.
- Tests: Night/{ClockTests,TuningTests}, Economy/{ShiftScoreTests,ComboAndStarTests,TipCalculatorTests}, Integration/{ShiftHarness,FullShiftTests,EventLog,EventContractTests,PhysicsAndTipTests,TuningSeederTests}, UI/{ShiftFlowTests,ResultsFormatterTests}, plus mechanical event and clock renames in Patrons, Audio and Core tests.
Why: chaos_redesign.md sections 3, 8 and 9. The mafia layer (debt, strikes, boss orders, phone UI, multi-night plan) is cut. A shift is 4 minutes from a ShiftConfig asset (length, star thresholds 150/300/450, $15 complaint penalty, combo cap 4x, pacing plan). ShiftClock counts real seconds down and publishes ShiftStarted, ShiftTick per game minute, ShiftLast30Seconds and ShiftEnded behind IGameClock. ShiftScore owns score, combo and complaints: a tip adds to the score and raises the combo, a $0 tip resets it, and either ticket timer running out (an angry patron) costs the penalty and resets it. The tip is base + speed + patience bonus + charge + clean, less damage stages, times the combo. Damage is an input defaulting to 0; nothing produces damage yet. Game minutes now default to one per real second, so patron windows (park 45, pickup 60, stay 20-45) read as seconds; arrivals are every 15-25 s. The Night assembly keeps its name (UncannyValet.Night) to avoid asmdef churn. The results screen shows stars, score and one Retry button (click, Enter or Space) that publishes RetryShiftRequested; ShiftFlow restarts the shift from a clean lot.
Tests: added or rewrote EditMode tests for the shift timer and clock, plan source and live tuning, combo, stars, damage stages, patience bonus, complaint rules, the full shift loop, shift flow and results text. NOT run: Unity could not be launched in this sandbox, so compilation and the whole suite are unverified.

CarChargeChanged and the timer events stay: TicketBoard, the info box and audio still use them.

### Decisions to know
- Plate drawn with the existing pixel kit (UI canvas projected from the car, like the info box), not a world sprite: the kit has the font, and the text is a cached string per ticket row so nothing allocates per frame; no per-plate texture is needed. It keeps a fixed pixel size (does not scale with camera zoom), under the car in screen space.
- Pickup flash: plate border plus glow, and a body tint pulse. A sprite-shaped silhouette outline was not done: it needs a flat silhouette texture (Read/Write sprites or a shader), which cannot be checked here.
- Car variety: Cars-Sheet has only 12 civilian and 2 EV top-down cells (all were already used; the rest are side views, ambulances or the fire truck). Variety now comes from adding the 2 police cells (same footprint), mirroring each sprite for half of the car ids, and the per-ticket tint. Ambulances (longer) and the fire truck are excluded.

=== docs/changelogs/pivot-visuals.md ===
# pivot-visuals changelog

## pivot-visuals: MetroCity art for the diner lot, cars and valet
Date: 2026-11-09
Files: Scripts/Lot/{LotArtConfig,DecorLayout,SpriteTiler,LotDecor,LotCurbArt,LotLayoutBuilder,SpotBuilder}.cs, Scripts/Cars/{CarSpriteEntry,CarArtConfig,CarArtMath,CarBodyArt,CarFactory,CarTraitLook}.cs, Scripts/Valet/{ValetFacing,ValetAnimMath,ValetArtConfig,ValetAnimator}.cs, Scripts/Bootstrap/GameBootstrap.cs, Editor/{BootstrapSceneBuilder}.cs, Editor/Art/{ArtSprites,ArtAssetUtil,LotArtBuilder,CarArtBuilder,ValetArtBuilder}.cs, Tests/EditMode/{Cars/CarArtMathTests,Valet/ValetAnimMathTests,Lot/DecorLayoutTests}.cs, docs/changelogs/pivot-visuals.md
Why: the human wants to see the imported MetroCity art (chaos_redesign.md pivot; sprite names from pivot-art.md). Unity could not be launched: nothing here was compiled or run.
Tests: three new EditMode fixtures (car art maths, valet animation maths, decor layout); not run.

### Lot (step 1)
- LotDecor (new, sibling of the Lot object, same origin, built at Awake) draws: grass, a south sidewalk, a 3 unit road with the dashed Road.png tile and code-drawn crosswalk bars (the Road.png crosswalk tiles only fit crossroads), a far sidewalk, a north sidewalk, the tan green-roof house as the diner, 8 trees (Tree-Sheet) and grass tufts. Placement is the pure DecorLayout around the largest lot frame (LotGeometry.Bounds).
- The lot logic is untouched. Visual changes: ground colour = asphalt sampled from Road.png, walls = hedge green, spots = painted white bays on the asphalt (pad takes the asphalt colour), curb = cobble tiles with a yellow edge and a dark plate behind CURB. Charging bays keep their blue paint.
- Camera: GameBootstrap widens the measured lot bounds vertically with LotDecor.ExtendView so the road and diner are in frame (zoom is capped at 16 by CameraFollow; beyond that it follows the valet).
- Sprites that are auto-sliced (Tiles, Buildings) are found by a pixel inside them (LotArtBuilder: cobble 264,40; house 1147,645; tufts), not by index.

### Cars (step 2)
- CarArtConfig holds 12 regular cells and 2 EV cells of Cars-Sheet (r0c0 r0c1 r0c5 r0c6 r1c1 r1c5 r2c0 r2c1 r2c4 r2c5 r3c0 r3c5; EV r1c0 r3c4). Each entry stores the opaque size and centre, measured by the editor builder. A car id picks its sprite by a stable hash.
- CarBodyArt (child "Body", order 10) scales the sprite uniformly so its opaque length equals the CarDefinition length, centred on the car. EVs swap to an EV sprite on TicketTraitsAssigned (no tint). The root rectangle is hidden, mirrors are skipped; mud, timer fill, charge bar and letter label are unchanged. Without a CarArtConfig the old rectangles still work.
- Per-sprite rotation offset: CarSpriteEntry rotation (0 or 180 only, because the car root is scaled non-uniformly). CarArtBuilder.RotationOffset (0) is applied to every entry.

### Valet (step 3)
- ValetAnimator stacks Model (skin row 0), Outfit1 and Hairs row 0 as three SpriteRenderers (orders 20 to 22), reads Rigidbody2D.linearVelocity and shows frame = floor(movingSeconds x 10 fps) mod 6 of the facing group, or frame 0 when still. Facing is the dominant velocity axis (ValetAnimMath). No allocations in Update.
- Groups: Down cols 0-5, Right 6-11, Up 12-17, Left 18-23; flip flags in ValetArtConfig. Valet scale is 1.4 (collider radius 0.3, about the old world size).
- GameBootstrap no longer paints the valet yellow when a ValetAnimator exists.

### Build
Setup > Build Everything now also creates Data/Lot/LotArtConfig, Data/Cars/CarArtConfig and Data/Valet/ValetArtConfig and wires them (factory, lot, decor, valet animator, bootstrap).

Wiring needed (integration/lead), in the Bootstrap scene or GameBootstrap:
- Add one GameObject with `PowerMeterController` (assign a `PowerMeterConfig` asset, create via Assets > Create > Uncanny Valet > Power Meter Config; defaults match the spec) and `PowerActivationController` (assign the meter controller). Both resolve `IEventBus` in Start, so they must start after the bus is registered. `PowerActivationController` registers `IWorldTime` in Start (FreezeTimeEffect); consumers should resolve it with `Services.TryGet` in their own Start or lazily.
- Optional: pass the config to `TuningSeeder.Seed(..., powers: config)`; without it the seeder uses config defaults.
- Optional: register an `IPowerInput` to replace the built-in Q / left shoulder `KeyboardPowerInput`.
- Valet adoption (later task): read `PowerActivationController.SpeedBoost.WalkMultiplier`.

Open seams and deviations:
- `PerfectPark` event (lot agent, issue #16) is not in Core yet: `PowerMeterController.AddPerfectPark()` is the named seam; subscribe to the event there when it lands.
- Activate input lives in the Powers module (`IPowerInput`) so the Valet module and `IInputSource` are untouched.
- Manager (`PowerStack`) publishes PowerActivated for every power, including Park Smash; the effects themselves stay free of events.
- Edited Bootstrap `TuningSeeder` and its asmdef (one optional parameter) because `TuningSeederTests` requires every catalog key to be seeded.

Tests: ModifierTestKit, Core/ModifierTests, Run/LotModifierChanceTests, PlayMode/ServiceModifierPlayModeTests, and every EditMode test that used the old traits.
Why: Issue #121 of epic #79. The hard-coded dirty and EV traits become two `ServiceModifierDefinition` assets (Muddy, cleared at the Wash station kind; NeedsCharge, cleared at Charging, starts 20% charged) on a list-based car state: `ModifierSet` in requests, events and snapshots, a list of `ModifierInstance` on each ticket with its own progress. Events `CarChargeChanged/CarFullyCharged/CarCleanChanged/CarCleaned` became `ModifierProgressChanged/ModifierCleared`, `TicketTraitsAssigned` became `TicketModifiersAssigned`. Arrival chances come from the lot (`LotDefinition` list of modifier and chance, seeded with the other per-lot pacing values before the dials; roll order is list order, one draw, at most one modifier per car); the two Car Mix dials and the shift plan's EV and wash chances are removed (their TuningKey numbers stay reserved as Retired...). Each modifier has its own pixel icon, word forms for the key board status (Washing, Washed, Charging, Charged) and clear time and bonus keys. A car holding two modifiers is tracked, cleared and reported per modifier (MuddyEv cars already do). The word Dirty is gone from code, UI and F2 labels (CarArchetype Dirty and DirtyEv are now Muddy and MuddyEv; DirWeightDirty and DirWeightDirtyEv are DirWeightMuddy and DirWeightMuddyEv, same numbers; the director profile fields keep their values through FormerlySerializedAs).
Tests: EditMode 1350/1350 (the old suite migrated, plus ModifierTests, LotModifierChanceTests, a two-modifier ticket, snapshot, workload and tip test, dial removal and key-number checks; the 200-seed Director soak test passes), PlayMode 30/30 (BootstrapSceneSmokeTests plus two new tests on the real training lot: catalog and chances registered and seeded, and a Muddy plus NeedsCharge car washed and charged end to end). RunBatch run: only the two new definition assets and the lots' chance lists changed; Bootstrap.unity, Lot_MidgardCrazy.unity and CarHandlingConfig.asset churn reverted.

## traffic: two-way ambient roads, density and merge yielding [Claude]
Date: 2026-11-11
Files: Scripts/Traffic/{AmbientDensity,AmbientTraffic,LaneNetwork,LaneSet,SpawnGate,TrafficLanes,ViewWindow,YieldZone}.cs (new); Scripts/Traffic/{AmbientVehicle,ExitTrip,LaneMotion,LaneOccupancy,MergeGapRule,TrafficConfig,TrafficLane,TrafficModule,TripContext,TripTuning}.cs; removed Scripts/Traffic/AmbientSpawner.cs; Core/Contracts/{IRoadLayout,GameRows}.cs; Core/Events/TuningKey.cs; Bootstrap/{TrafficTuningLink,TuningSeeder,GameBootstrap}.cs (traffic wiring only); Data/Traffic/TrafficConfig.asset; Tests/EditMode/Traffic/{AmbientDensityTests,AmbientTrafficTests,PatronAmongAmbientTests,SpawnGateTests,ViewWindowTests,YieldZoneTests}.cs (new), {EntryRetargetTests,TripObstacleTests,TripTests}.cs, Tests/EditMode/Integration/{TrafficTuningLinkTests,TuningSeederTests}.cs; removed AmbientSpawnerTests.cs
Why: Issue #75 (Refs #75). Both roads now carry ambient cars both ways (two new right-to-left lanes, TrafficLane.EastBack/TopBack; the patron lanes and routes are unchanged). The random-rate spawner is replaced by AmbientDensity: 3 to 4 ambient cars on screen (counted against the camera's visible rectangle, ViewWindow, cached camera), spawned just beyond the screen edge they drive in from, only into a free gap (SpawnGate), removed once off screen, random speeds, gaps, looks and lanes. F2 rows: fewest and target cars on screen replace cars per minute (TuningKey.TrafficAmbientPerMinute removed, TrafficMinOnScreen and TrafficTargetOnScreen appended); speed scale key unchanged. A delivered car waiting to merge asks the exit lane to yield (YieldZone, a virtual stopped car one merge gap before the merge point): ambient cars that can still stop brake smoothly through the existing car-following, so the gap opens.

RunBatch ran (Bootstrap.unity fileID churn reverted). EditMode 1159/1159 and PlayMode 16/16 pass.

# ui-status changelog

## ui-status: CAR SLOTS rows show a status word
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Scripts/UI/Status/{CarStatus,ICarStatusRule,CarStatusResolver,CarStatusTable,CustAngryStatusRule,PickUpStatusRule,WashingStatusRule,ChargingStatusRule,WashedStatusRule,ChargedStatusRule}.cs, Scripts/UI/{TicketRow,TicketBoard}.cs, Scripts/UI/Hud/KeyBoardPainter.cs, Tests/EditMode/UI/CarStatusTests.cs
Why: human request (design.md A4 / style guide rev 6): each CAR SLOTS row shows Parked, Washing, Washed, Charging, Charged, Pick Up or Cust. Angry instead of the dash and the patience bar; the spot number and the pickup bell stay. The resolver takes the first applying rule of an ordered list (Cust. Angry, Pick Up, Washing, Charging, Washed, Charged, else Parked); a new status is one enum id, one rule class, one table entry. TicketRow now remembers the station the car is in (CarEnteredStation / CarLeftStation); no new gameplay state. "Cust. Angry" is 65 px wide and the row has 56 px left, so the table has a short label (Angry) used only when the full one does not fit.
Tests: CarStatusTests (priorities, station transitions, table completeness, abbreviation, an extra rule joining the list, board tracks stations).

## ui-status: licence plate shows only once the car is parked
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Scripts/UI/TicketRow.cs, Scripts/UI/Hud/WorldTicketPainter.cs, Tests/EditMode/UI/TicketBoardTests.cs
Why: human saw a plate at the screen edge before the car was in view (design.md A4). The plate is drawn by the UI world painter (not a Cars component) and was gated only on HasPlate, with its position clamped to the canvas, so a plate for an off-screen car pinned itself to the edge. New TicketRow.ShowsPlate = HasPlate && Placed, the same Placed flag the CAR SLOTS list uses (CarParked or CarEnteredStation); the plate-assigned event still only fills the row text.
Tests: TicketBoardTests.PlateShowsOnlyOnceTheCarIsParked (regression: hidden while queued and after CarArrived, shown after CarParked), PlateShowsOnceTheCarEntersAStation.

## ui-status: pickup emotions on a patron bubble, the plate and the row
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Scripts/UI/Mood/{Emotion,EmotionRule,EmotionStyle,HudEmotionConfig,MoodBubbleLayout}.cs, Scripts/UI/Hud/{PatronMoodPainter,PickupMark,PlateLayout,Alarm,WorldTicketPainter,KeyBoardPainter}.cs, Scripts/UI/{GameUi}.cs, Scripts/UI/Status/{CustAngryStatusRule,CarStatusResolver}.cs, Editor/BootstrapSceneBuilder.cs, Data/UI/HudEmotionConfig.asset, Scenes/Bootstrap.unity (regenerated: GameUi gets the config reference; the file shows id churn), Tests/EditMode/UI/{EmotionTests,PickupMarkTests,HudRulesTests,PlateUiTests,CarStatusTests}.cs
Why: human request (design.md A4 pickup urgency). One pure EmotionRule (patience fraction to Happy / Impatient / Angry; angry also when expired) feeds (a) a small pixel chat bubble with a face over the waiting patron, placed through IPatronLocator so it follows them and vanishes when they stop waiting or the ticket closes, (b) the plate flash colour and speed, (c) the CAR SLOTS highlight, bell and the Cust. Angry status. Colours green / yellow / red, flash 1.5 / 3 / 7 Hz, thresholds 0.6 / 0.85 (the old amber and danger levels) all live in the new HudEmotionConfig asset with [Min]/[Range]/[Tooltip]. The old cyan/amber/orange-red plate flash, FlashPeriod, the patience constants and the patience bar maths in Alarm are removed (dead code). The Cars-module body tint pulse (CarFlashConfig) is untouched and still uses cyan/amber/orange-red.
Tests: EmotionTests (thresholds, expiry, rates and colours, bubble faces, Cust. Angry follows Angry), PickupMarkTests rewritten for emotion tint and speed. Full EditMode run at this commit.

## ui-status: split TicketBoard and GameUi under the 200-line limit
Date: 2026-11-10
Files: Scripts/UI/{TicketBoard,TicketNeedsTracker,GameUi}.cs, Scripts/UI/Hud/PlayingHud.cs
Why: CLAUDE.md file-size limit, no behaviour change. TicketNeedsTracker takes the EV, dirty, charge and wash-progress handlers (and the shared FindByCar); PlayingHud owns the ticket board, the in-shift painters and the hint trackers that GameUi built and drew directly.
Tests: full EditMode run, 737 passed, 0 failed.

## valet: Thunder stride lasts 5 s and glows while it runs [Claude]
Date: 2026-11-16
Files: Valet: ThunderAura, ThunderAuraClock, ThunderAuraTiming, ThunderAuraLook, AuraTint, AuraGlow (new), ThunderDash (owns the glow, ShiftEnded clears it); Powers: PowerDefaults (boost 5 s level 1, 7 s level 2); Data/Powers/ThunderStrideDefinition.asset (same durations); Tests: ThunderAuraClockTests, ThunderAuraPlayModeTests (new), ThunderStrideTests (timings, no-stack test).
Why: issue #150. The boost ended too fast and was hard to see. It now lasts 5 s (7 s at level 2, still tunable in F2 Advanced) and the valet, or the car he drives, gets a pulsing golden tint (MaterialPropertyBlock), a soft glow under it and a steady spray of lightning sparks for the whole boost, blinking in the last second. The cooldown still starts when the boost ends and a fire during the boost is refused, so nothing stacks. The glow clears on PowerEnded, ShiftEnded, disable and destroy, and follows car enter and exit.
Tests: EditMode 1916 passed (clock range, warning window, clear, restart); PlayMode 83 passed (tint and glow on, off at PowerEnded and ShiftEnded, no leaked glow object); RunBatch ok (scene and asset churn reverted).
